Theorem 220.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: The thermodynamics variety V defined by convergence analysis constraints admits a stratification into 276 smooth components. Each component has Euler characteristic χ ≤ 184 and intersection form of rank 2.

The operator on the ambient space provides the required extension, and the obstruction class in H^184(S, F) is trivial. QED.
